        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf14', get_index_2)

        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')

        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-06, torch.float32)

        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)

        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('arg6_1', get_index_4)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(load_4, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.float16)

class op49_loop_body:

    index0 = 8388608*p0 + 512*p1 + p2
    index1 = p2
    index2 = 32*p0 + ((p2//16))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf4', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('arg4_1', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf36', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('arg13_1', get_index_3)
        add_1 = ops.add(load_2, load_3)
        add_2 = ops.add(add, add_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float16)
        mul = ops.mul(add_2, constant)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(mul, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.float16)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf46', get_index_4)
        sub = ops.sub(to_dtype, load_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf47', get_index_5)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(262144.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_5, constant_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(1e-06, torch.float32)
        add_3 = ops.add(truediv, constant_2)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_3)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('arg14_1', get_index_6)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(load_6, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.float16)

        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')

        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(load_7, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.float16)
        add_4 = ops.add(mul_2, to_dtype_2)
        to_dtype_3 = ops.to_dtype(add_4, torch.float16, src_dtype = torch.float32)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf49', get_index_8, to_dtype_3, None)
        return store

    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf371', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('arg114_1', get_index_1)

        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf378', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf379', get_index_3)

        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-06, torch.float32)

        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
